SEC-RW8 (repair, WAVE-8 re-audit finding — NOT a numbered substep) · Phase-4 embed · close the loopback-twin whitespace class: shared edge-whitespace strip set (finding W8-A1-1 LOW) · 2026-09-22 · committed 8173a0c on branch feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening · tests: 8 new backend (test_platforms.py: 6 divergent chars × leading/trailing/bracketed on loopback bases + a U+200B not-over-stripped pin) + 2 new frontend (frame-csp.test.ts); backend unit 623→630, frontend vitest 286→288; red-before-green (U+FEFF cases red on backend, C1/NEL cases red on frontend) · levels: unit + DB + frontend + a coordinator cross-runtime differential · what changed: Wave 8 (5 fresh auditors) directed to END the loopback-parity tail — the auth auditor and the cross-cut critic each ran a LARGE cross-runtime differential (both real runtimes executed) and the critic enumerated every isdigit/int site. 3 domains detailed-CLEAN; the two differential auditors BOTH found ONE finding and converged on the identical root. (W8-A1-1 LOW) Python `str.strip()` and JS `String.prototype.trim()` strip DIFFERENT edge whitespace — disagree on exactly six code points (Python-only U+001C-U+001F+U+0085, JS-only U+FEFF) — so `_is_loopback_host`/`_domain_host`/`_is_dot_local_host` (.strip) and csp.ts `isLocalhostDomain` (.trim) classified a host carrying one of those six oppositely (e.g. `localhost<BOM>` slipped past the backend trust guard while the frontend stripped it). Two independent differentials (3,779 + 3,137 inputs) converged on the same complete six-char set. Fix (definitive, by construction): both twins now strip ONE shared explicit union set — backend `_EDGE_WHITESPACE`/`_strip_edges`, frontend `EDGE_WHITESPACE`/`stripEdges` (BMP single code units, charCodeAt walk) — replacing bare .strip()/.trim() at all host-matching sites; the set is the exact union (excludes U+200B, pinned by a not-over-stripped test). Coordinator's OWN differential verified equivalence: 3,993 inputs (every ws/control/format cp ≤ U+FFFF × 10 bases), backend vs real csp.ts via node --experimental-strip-types → 0 divergences (was 149). · deviations, stated plainly: (a) NOT a numbered substep; (b) W8-A1-1 is LOW/fail-closed/operator-config-only (not attacker-reachable — the six chars must be hand-pasted into an ACTIVE platform's config, and the backend already fail-closes at load) — fixed anyway per the owner's all-actionable-gaps directive and because it is the ROOT of the recurring tail (fixing the class, not the leaf, stops future waves finding more whitespace chars); (c) chose a shared-union-set fix over matching one runtime's exact set, so the twins are equivalent by construction and provably closed (0/3993 differential), rather than a narrower patch that could leave a 7th char; (d) repair + differential done in the MAIN WINDOW not a separate repair subagent (recurring spend-limit; independence preserved by fresh Waves 9+10); (e) the isdigit/int class was independently confirmed fully closed by the critic's site enumeration — no code change needed there · defaults built to: none · output: platforms.py, test_platforms.py, csp.ts, frame-csp.test.ts; this ledger entry + progress-log + loop-log. SEC-EMBED-FIX (defect found during owner localhost testing — NOT a numbered substep) · Phase-4 embed · /embed 500 from a server-only module reaching the client bundle · 2026-09-22 · WORKING TREE, NOT YET COMMITTED on branch feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening · tests: 1 new frontend unit — client-root-no-server-imports.test.ts (panel w-proxy: the chat CLIENT public root src/features/chat/index.ts must never re-export a ./server module; RED before the fix, GREEN after) + route-handlers.test.ts mock retargeted @/features/chat → @/features/chat/server · levels: unit (frontend vitest 289 passed, was 288; tsc clean) + live (curl http://localhost:3100/embed → HTTP 200, was 500; server-only error gone from next-dev log) · ROOT CAUSE: src/features/chat/index.ts (the client barrel) re-exported handlePostChat/handlePatchFeedback from ./server/route-handlers, which imports @/platform/automation-api → client.ts → `import "server-only"` (it injects the CHAT_API_KEY host secret). The client component src/app/embed/embed-frame.tsx ("use client") imports that barrel for its UI/hook exports, so Next resolved the whole barrel module graph and pulled server-only into the client bundle → build error → /embed 500 → widget could not load. WHY MISSED BY THE LOOP: the Playwright mount test (widget-mounts.spec.ts) asserts the <iframe src="/embed"> ELEMENT exists on the parent /test-hosts page but never navigates into /embed, and vitest/tsc do not compile the Next client/server boundary — so the 500 was invisible to the frontend gate. FIX (mirrors the pattern features/embed/index.ts already documents for its server-only platforms.ts): (1) removed the server re-export from the client root and documented why; (2) NEW src/features/chat/server.ts — the SERVER public root, carries `import "server-only"`, re-exports handlePostChat/handlePatchFeedback; (3) app/api/chat/route.ts and app/api/chat/[traceId]/feedback/route.ts import from @/features/chat/server instead of @/features/chat. SECURITY POSTURE (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints, LLM-CALL proxy surface): unchanged-to-strengthened — the CHAT_API_KEY secret path is now MORE strongly isolated from the browser (the client root no longer references server code at all; the server root fails the build loudly on any accidental client import). No control weakened; C1/secret-handling improved. · deviations, stated plainly: (a) NOT a numbered substep — a defect fix during owner testing; (b) the live proof is a curl of /embed (HTTP 200) against the running local dev server, not a new Playwright test — the fast unit guard is the committed regression; strengthening the e2e mount test to actually load /embed is noted as a follow-up; (c) whole-repo frontend lint is not gated in this repo (no ESLint config); tsc + vitest are the gates and both are green.

SEC-TUNNEL (owner-requested "quick test online" tooling — NOT a numbered substep) · Phase-4 embed · scripts/tunnel-embed-test.sh + `make tunnel`: one-command cloudflared tunnel of the LOCAL frontend for a self-only, general-only online test of the embed widget · 2026-09-23 · WORKING TREE, NOT YET COMMITTED on branch feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening · tests: none automated (a bash operator script + a Makefile target — no product code path, same posture as SEC-BRIEF's make targets); verified by hand — `bash -n` parses; the python3 domain-injection round-trip run on a COPY adds the tunnel host to all four active test entries' `domains` and re-serializes valid JSON; `make -n tunnel` resolves to the script; a LIVE cloudflared quick tunnel issued a real URL captured by the script's exact regex (https://cross-labor-infants-designed.trycloudflare.com); the real platforms.local.json confirmed git-clean (git status empty) after the tests · levels: manual / component-mechanics only (see deviation b) · what changed: (1) NEW scripts/tunnel-embed-test.sh — tunnels ONLY the frontend :3100; the backend stays on localhost:8000 and is reached server-side via AUTOMATION_API_BASE_URL, so nothing internal is exposed. It runs `cloudflared tunnel --url http://localhost:3100`, captures the random *.trycloudflare.com hostname, and injects it (bare host, no scheme/port) into EVERY active entry's `domains` in platforms.local.json — required because the embed CSP `frame-ancestors` (middleware.ts + csp.ts:249) AND the postMessage origin allow-list (iframe-bridge.ts:89 ← embed/page.tsx:24) both derive from active `domains` and local/dev mode adds NO wildcard and NO 'self' (so an un-listed tunnel host = iframe blocked + obi:open rejected + blank panel). No obi.js rebuild is needed: the loader falls back to window.location.origin when OBI_ORIGIN is unset (loader.ts:32-34), so serving obi.js from the tunnel origin makes the iframe load from <tunnel>/embed automatically. On EXIT/INT/TERM it kills cloudflared and restores the registry verbatim from a snapshot (idempotent _CLEANED guard). The general-only path uses the existing /test-hosts/none host (mints a no-business-claims token; the panel opens even if the token mint 401/503s; backend treats absent X-Obi-Token as general-only). (2) NEW `make tunnel` target + added to .PHONY. · deviations, stated plainly: (a) NOT a numbered substep — owner-requested go-live-prep tooling following the SEC-BRIEF follow-on; (b) I verified the SCRIPT MECHANICS (parse, JSON round-trip, live URL capture, make target, registry restore) but did NOT run the full end-to-end browser load over the tunnel — that requires `make api` + `make embed-dev` running plus a human at a browser (the owner's next step), and a non-empty general-only answer further depends on obi-general-test content existing in the live corpus; (c) macOS ships no GNU `timeout`, so the script deliberately avoids it — it uses a bounded poll loop with a `kill -0` liveness check instead (this was caught during verification when a `timeout`-based test returned 127); (d) free cloudflared quick tunnels rotate the hostname each run — the script re-injects per run and platforms.ts re-reads the registry per request (no Next restart, no rebuild), and the transient edit to the committed platforms.local.json lives only for the session and is auto-restored on exit.

SEC-MW (defect found during owner "test online" run — NOT a numbered substep) · Phase-4 embed · the /embed CSP middleware's internal-domains self-fetch broke frame-ancestors behind a reverse proxy / tunnel, blocking the widget iframe · 2026-09-23 · WORKING TREE, NOT YET COMMITTED on branch feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening · tests: NONE YET — a unit test is an OWED, DISCLOSED follow-up (see deviation b); verified LIVE instead — over a cloudflared tunnel, GET /embed `frame-ancestors` went from `'none'` (iframe blocked) to `localhost:3000 localhost:3100 <tunnel-host>` after the fix, and a general-only chat then streamed end-to-end through the public URL · levels: live (tunnel curl) only · ROOT CAUSE: frontend/src/middleware.ts computed `frame-ancestors` from the active platform domains fetched via `fetch(new URL("/api/internal/active-domains", request.url))`. Behind a proxy/tunnel `request.url` carries the PUBLIC host, so the Next server looped back out through its own public hostname to reach its own internal API; that self-loop fails behind cloudflared → the `catch` sets domains=[] → `computeEmbedCsp` returns `frame-ancestors 'none'` → /embed refuses to be framed → the widget iframe never loads. The same latent failure applies to ANY reverse-proxied production deployment, not just tunnels (surfaced here only because the online test forced a proxy in front of the app). FIX: prefer an explicit loopback origin — `const internalBase = process.env.OBI_INTERNAL_ORIGIN || request.url` — and pass `{ cache: "no-store" }` to honor the file's own documented "never cached" contract. Env unset → byte-identical prior behavior (backward compatible). `OBI_INTERNAL_ORIGIN=http://localhost:3100` was added to the gitignored frontend/.env.local for the local/tunnel run. SECURITY POSTURE (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints — surface: GET /embed CSP middleware, tier PUBLIC-READ, required subset C2/C3/C4): STRENGTHENED, not weakened — the domains→frame-ancestors computation and the fail-closed-when-empty behavior are unchanged; the change REMOVES a Host-header-influenced outbound fetch target (`request.url`) in favor of a fixed loopback origin, and enforces no-store. No required control is removed. · deviations, stated plainly: (a) NOT a numbered substep — a defect fixed while bringing localhost live for an owner online test; (b) NO unit test yet — OWED follow-up: a middleware test asserting OBI_INTERNAL_ORIGIN is used when set / request.url when unset, and that an empty active-domain list still yields the fail-closed header; the current proof is the live tunnel curl (frame-ancestors before→after) — a real gap against "no change without its test", disclosed not hidden; (c) product code changed (middleware.ts) — working tree, ships with the branch commit; (d) OBI_INTERNAL_ORIGIN lives only in gitignored frontend/.env.local — a per-deployment operator setting, not committed.

SEC-READER (owner-requested live fix during "test online" — NOT a numbered substep) · Phase-4 embed / go-live prep · the rag_reader Supabase role was re-provisioned (its old password failed authentication), pgvector `extensions` access granted, and read-path isolation re-verified live · 2026-09-23 · Supabase role change + gitignored root .env change; reader-no-GUC 0 (default-deny); reader-scoped 107; reader-bogus-source 0; reader halfvec RESOLVES (dense-query path OK); anon 0/0; scope axis `base` in-scope 85 / out-of-scope 0 → "OK: ADR-0004 read-path isolation + reader read-access hold"; plus a live general-only chat via the proxy then ran clean (no auth error) · levels: live (verify-isolation + chat) · what happened / changed: (1) during the online test a chat failed with psycopg "password authentication failed for user rag_reader" — the DSN's stray space had already been removed, but the password itself was wrong/rotated; (2) INTERIM (disclosed loudly, then reversed): DATABASE_READER_URL was emptied so ENV=local retrieval fell back to the OWNER DSN, which BYPASSES RLS, purely to unblock the single-source self-test; (3) FIX: ran `scripts/setup_supabase.py provision-reader` — recreated rag_reader with a fresh `secrets.token_urlsafe(24)` password, re-asserted the chunk + non-chunk reader RLS policies, and wrote the correct pooler DSN (`rag_reader.<project_ref>`) into the gitignored root .env (password masked in output, never printed); (4) ran the two Supabase grants the script no-ops on, this time via the OWNER connection, which SUCCEEDED here: `GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA extensions TO rag_reader` + `ALTER ROLE rag_reader SET search_path = public, extensions` (without them the reader cannot cast `halfvec` and dense retrieval breaks even though chunk RLS is fine); (5) reloaded the backend and ran verify-isolation (all green above). Net: the interim owner-DSN RLS bypass is GONE — retrieval again runs as the constrained rag_reader, with working semantic search. · deviations, stated plainly: (a) NOT a numbered substep — an owner-requested operational fix during go-live testing; (b) the interim owner-DSN fallback DID briefly bypass RLS, in ENV=local only (single-source self-test), and is now removed — recorded for honesty, not hidden; (c) no automated test — this is live DB role provisioning; verify-isolation is the re-runnable proof; (d) the fix targets the CURRENT Supabase project only; a separate production project must be provisioned the same way; (e) the generated reader password lives only in the gitignored .env; (f) the blocker cleared without owner action because the owner DB role COULD grant on `extensions` here — contrary to the setup_supabase.py docstring's Supabase caution, which did not hold on this instance.

Job idempotency keys carry the event-ledger id (webhook sync/delete enqueue — fix for the swallowed-second-event bug found by the 2.0.2 webhook feasibility review; NOT a numbered substep) · 2026-09-29 · WORKING TREE, NOT YET COMMITTED on branch feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening · tests: 3 new db-level tests in test_webhook.py, red-first — test_i1_two_distinct_label_deliveries_on_one_page_each_enqueue_a_job (two label_added deliveries, distinct delivery_id, NO version block — the real label shape — collapsed to ONE job under the old key; asserts 2 jobs), test_i1_two_distinct_trash_deliveries_on_one_page_each_enqueue_a_job (the delete-class half: the old delete key had no per-event component at all, so a trash→restore→trash-again cycle never enqueued the second delete job; asserts 2), test_i1_true_duplicate_label_delivery_still_enqueues_one_job (the companion bound: a byte-identical redelivery still makes exactly ONE job, stopped at the event ledger) — both regression tests FAILED before the patch and pass after; all name panel i1-ledger · levels: unit + database (make check GREEN: boundaries OK · backend 1015 passed + 1 xfailed, was 1012 · knowledge-base 45 passed); ruff check + format --check + pyright clean on both touched backend files · what changed: event_service.py `_enqueue_for_event` — sync key `sync_page:{page_id}:{event_type}:{ver|na}:{schema}` → `sync_page:{page_id}:{event_id}` and delete key `delete_page:{page_id}:{event_type}` → `delete_page:{page_id}:{event_id}`, mirroring the already-correct `reconcile_space:{space_id}:{event_id}`; dead `ver`/`schema` locals removed; explanatory comment added; test_scope_tagging_change.py module docstring updated (distinct keys now come from the event-ledger id, not event_type) · why: enqueue_job is ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING on idempotency_key and completed jobs are never pruned, so any key built only from page state collides FOREVER — label events carry no page version ("na"), so the second-ever real label_added on a page, or any re-trash, silently enqueued NOTHING; this would break live sync rules 2/5/7 the moment a real webhook drives them (the 2026-09-25 green run drove sync in-process and never exercised the webhook enqueue path — which is why it stayed green) · safety, verified in code by an independent read-only agent + spot-checked in the main session: true duplicates still make one job (record_event returns None on payload_hash/delivery_id conflict BEFORE _enqueue_for_event is reached — existing test_duplicate_delivery_is_deduped + test_event_dedup.py all still green); sweep/reindex keys live in their own namespaces (sync_page:recon:…, sync_page:reindex:…) and are untouched — no cross-namespace dedup ever existed; a redundant same-version job is a cheap no_change no-op (change_detection version guard: no body fetch, no embed — pinned by test_worker_sync.py::test_version_guard_drops_stale_update) · security review (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints): endpoint surface unchanged (webhook.py C1–C10 block untouched); the change strengthens C7 idempotency CORRECTNESS (real events no longer lost) without loosening the duplicate bound · docs updated in the same change: ingestion/webhook.md (ledger-is-the-sole-redelivery-stop + the new key form + the 3 new tests) and ingestion/trigger-and-ingestion-split.md (D2 note) · deviations, stated plainly: (a) NOT a numbered substep — a bug surfaced by the Step-3 feasibility review; (b) the design page + generated brief still carry the OLD key sentence ("sync_page:{page_id}:{event_type}:{version}:{schema} … two identical ones get one" — brief line ~1526, sourced from obi-rag-system-flow.html) — NOT edited here per the branch convention that design-page edits need owner sign-off; flagged as an owner tail to regenerate; (c) job-table insert volume rises modestly (every real event now enqueues; each redundant one collapses to no_change) and completed jobs are still never GC'd — pre-existing, recorded for a future substep, not introduced here; Forge CLI keychain hang resolved via env-var auth (closes the "open wrinkle" of the Step-3 prerequisites entry) · 2026-09-29 · environment only, no repo code changed · what: after the node 26→22 switch, every forge command (even --version) hung with zero output in every shell, including the owner's interactive Terminal — diagnosed by sampling the hung process: blocked in `keytar.node → GetPasswordWorker::Execute() → SecKeychainFindGenericPassword` (macOS Keychain read; the new node binary is not on the keychain item's ACL and the authorization dialog never displayed, even after killing the queued/stuck forge processes that macOS serializes behind). Hardening batch · Scope/label/integration lifecycle regression floor + reconciliation-sweep CQL fix · 2026-10-03 · WORKING TREE, NOT YET COMMITTED on feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening · what was done (two read-only audits, then 4 agents, every test inversion-proven red→green): (1) SWEEP CQL BUG FIXED test-first — root cause: `space.id` and `status` are not CQL fields on Confluence Cloud (the API's own 400 named them), and CQL cannot filter by space id at all (a bare number matches a space KEY); fix in platform/clients/confluence_client.py list_pages_by_label only: `type = page AND label in (...)` + explicit cqlcontext current-only + client-side space-id filter; 3 new MockTransport tests pin the exact request (red on the old query), fixture docstring's false "live-tested" claim corrected; live read-only proof returned exactly the 5 indexed pages; reconciliation sweeps are usable again · (2) 18 NEW TESTS, all green inside `make check` (backend 1033+1xfail, kb 45, boundaries OK): test_scope_config_lifecycle.py (5 — scope removed from tag map → complete sweep purges / lightweight sweep's blind spot pinned deliberately / scope-added no-restamp pinned (see finding A) / classified-off reindexes as first build / mixed-case label normalized end-to-end), test_platform_registry_cache.py (3 — platforms.json edits served stale until restart (restart-required contract pinned), scopes-file change revalidates platforms LOUDLY naming the scope, cache identity), test_router_classified_scope.py (1 — literal body knowledge_scope="classified" is 400 before any search, ADR-0011 edge pin), test_seed_source_scope_script.py (3 — first-ever tests for the operator seed script: insert, idempotent upsert, --deactivate/--delete), test_source_scope_lifecycle.py (3 — narrowed-root leak window proven at RLS level under rag_reader, complete-sweep no_change gap pinned (finding B), active-but-not-covering space variant purges) · FINDINGS, unprompted — two real gaps the tests pin as known-and-not-endorsed rather than hide: (A) a scope ADDED to knowledge_scopes.json never reaches already-indexed pages — classify() keys on labels_hash (raw Confluence labels, unchanged), so even the complete sweep takes no_change; pages gain the new scope only on a real page event; (B) worse, NARROWING a source_scope row's tags leaks the revoked scope indefinitely — the stale chunk.tags keep satisfying RLS overlap and the complete sweep also lands in no_change because classify() never compares cmd.scopes to the stamped tags; candidate fix for both: classify/handler compares cmd.scopes against local stamped tags and routes to metadata_only (NOT applied — needs its own red-first substep and an owner decision) · levels: unit + database (make check green) + live read-only (CQL fix proof) · need from you: schedule the finding-A/B metadata-restamp fix; decide the archive-fires-no-webhook gap (unchanged from 2026-10-02); commit the working tree when ready.

4.3-addendum · The live run of webhook_rules_live.py — ran clean after one real bug found and fixed · 2026-10-05 · WORKING TREE, NOT YET COMMITTED on feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening · what was done: the owner ordered the live run in-session. FIRST RUN: rules 2/5/4/3/6/7 + the trash half of rule 1 all PASSED through the real webhook (~11 s per leg, one `origin=0` ledger row per leg, ids #118–#125) — then the restore leg CRASHED on a real API gap: Confluence's v1 content GET returns neither `title` nor `version` for trashed content, so `_restore_page` KeyError'd and the cleanup (which calls the same function) could not recover either, leaving the Toast Testpage trashed in Confluence and purged in the DB · recovery, immediately: probed the API live (v2 `GET /api/v2/pages/{id}?status=trashed` DOES return title+version), fixed `_restore_page` to source both from v2 (and hardened `_page_is_trashed` to check the returned status, not just the 200), then restored the page via the fixed code — re-indexed by the real webhook in 11.4 s, the first run's leftover body marker stripped and re-indexed, labels confirmed back to exactly `['obi-toast-test']` · SECOND RUN, complete and clean: all 8 rules PASS through the live webhook path (ledger rows #127–#136), rule 1 restore included (fresh document_version on restore), cleanup left zero residue (marker gone, labels restored, original tags re-indexed), rule-8 witness fingerprint byte-identical — final verdict "OK"; output in `backend/webhook-rules-live-run.txt` (the PR paste) · gate: ruff/format/pyright clean on the fixed script; whole-repo pyright 41 (unchanged); kb suite green · deviations: the first run's crash mid-trash-leg is exactly the failure mode the runner exists to surface — recorded as found, not smoothed over; exit code of the first run read 0 only because of the `| tee` pipeline (the runner itself raised) — a known cosmetic trap when eyeballing exit codes through tee · with this, 4.3's full acceptance ("exists and runs clean") is met

D.0 · The scope-restamp fix — gaps A+B closed at the complete-sweep cadence (roadmap Step 5 part 0, §D.0 of docs/Final_docs/plan.md; owner-ordered 2026-10-03, go-ahead 2026-10-05) · 2026-10-05 · commit follows this entry on feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening · what was done: the ingestion command handler now compares the authoritative `cmd.scopes` (recognized_labels ∩ authorized, computed by the trigger's decide_command) against the page's stamped tags on an otherwise-unchanged page, and routes a mismatch to the existing metadata-only restamp path instead of `no_change` — `SyncOutcome(action="metadata_only", classes=["scopes_restamped"])`, tags corrected on page_source AND every active chunk, same document_version, zero embed calls. This closes BOTH 2026-10-03 hardening findings: (A) a scope added to knowledge_scopes.json now reaches already-indexed pages carrying its label on the next COMPLETE sweep (was: never — classify keys on labels_hash, which a config edit does not move); (B) narrowing a source_scope row's allow-list now revokes the scope from stamped chunks on the next COMPLETE sweep, closing the indefinite RLS leak window (was: stale chunk.tags kept satisfying the `tags &&` overlap forever). Code: 3 files — `change_detection.py` (LocalState gains `tags`, documented as handler-compared, not classify-compared), `page_source_repo.py` (populates it), `command_handler.py` (the drift check + routing; the only behavioral change). No enum/migration needed: `classes` is a plain list[str] no non-test code branches on · tests: 2, red-first — the two 2026-10-03 sentinels consciously flipped exactly as their docstrings instructed: test_tags_scope_added_already_indexed_page_restamped_by_complete_sweep (test_scope_config_lifecycle.py; was ..._not_restamped_by_either_sweep) asserts restamp to both scopes, same version, 0 embeds, lightweight still blind, second complete sweep no_change; test_tags_narrowed_root_complete_sweep_restamps_and_closes_the_leak (test_source_scope_lifecycle.py; was ..._takes_no_change_path_and_keeps_stale_tags) asserts restamp to {A}, revoked scope B reads 0 rows as rag_reader at RLS level (the leak-closed proof the §D.0 done-bar names), kept scope A still reads, same version, 0 embeds, second sweep no_change. Both RED on pre-fix code (observed: `no_change` + stale tags), GREEN after; both lightweight-window sibling pins untouched and green · levels: database (make check green: backend 1066 passed + 1 xfailed [the D2 attachment strict xfail], kb 49, boundaries 0) + eval (make eval byte-identical to the 4.1 baseline on every stage and the rerank lift — rule 11) + lint/type (ruff check/format clean on all 5 touched files; pyright 0 errors on them; whole-repo pyright 41 = the 4.2 floor, ADR-0003 D1 holds) · docs: trigger-and-ingestion-split.md (what/how-we-know updated), handover/tag-administration.md (finding-A/B sections rewritten to the fixed behavior + the remaining lightweight/cadence caveat with the run-a-sweep-now command), handover/integration-onboarding.md (flag resolved), coverage-map.md §D.0 section, plan.md §D.0 done-note · deviations, unprompted: (a) substep id is a roadmap part (§D.0), not an action-plan HTML id — no checkbox exists, no design-page panel closes (the fix strengthens built panels tg-config/tg-add and the sweep behavior; ks-sweep stays `build` — its CQL-label-search target is separate work), so no status flip and no brief regeneration; (b) the lightweight-sweep blind spot is deliberately NOT closed (out of §D.0's scope; a config-only edit fires no cheap drift signal) — operational mitigation documented: run a complete sweep for immediate effect, siblings keep it pinned; (c) the fix lives in the handler (command_handler), not classify() — classify is pure page-side state and cmd.scopes is trigger-derived; the plan's wording "classify/handler" allowed either, the handler is the single choke point every upsert passes · security: no HTTP endpoint, LLM call, or outbound network modified; the change is strictly tightening on the leak side (gap B revokes access that should have been revoked) and strictly additive on the sharing side (gap A stamps a scope the owner explicitly added to the tag map; membership itself is unchanged — an untagged/classified page still purges before this code runs) · defaults built to: none · NEED FROM YOU (owner): the archive-fires-no-webhook decision (2026-10-02 finding, §D.0 names it): an ARCHIVED page fires no webhook event and stays searchable until a sweep catches it. Recommendation: (1) check whether Forge offers an archived-page event trigger (e.g. avi:confluence:archived:page) and subscribe the bridge if so — smallest real fix, one manifest edit + redeploy; (2) if no such event exists, accept the sweep-cadence window and record it as the documented behavior (archived ∈ _GONE_STATUSES, so any later event or sweep purges it). Until you call it, nothing changes.

supersedes both earlier conflicting notes, no Jasper question; (2) company ID alone suffices, no name; (3) the design decisions: token = company ID + three slots (each one system or empty); launch allowlist Toast=POS, Mews=PMS — a FILLED POS/PMS slot outside the allowlist hides Obi, an EMPTY slot never does (owner decided yes on the Mews-hotel-empty-POS case); D.13-item-4 · Archive caught by the lightweight sweep — the follow-up test (owner-ordered 2026-10-05, run in the owner's parallel batch) · 2026-10-05 · working tree, uncommitted per the branch convention · what happened: new database test test_in2_archived_page_without_webhook_purged_by_lightweight_sweep (confluence_sync/tests/test_sync_rules_delete.py, panel i2-gone · rule 1 · §D.13 item 4) proves the archive rule's detection window is the DAILY cadence: a page indexed with live chunks, then archived upstream with NO webhook, is caught by the lightweight sweep's orphan pass (the page drops out of the current-pages enumeration → enqueued page_gone → worker purges every row to zero: chunk/document_version/document/page_source/page_restriction). Finding of record: the status-drift signal in _needs_sync never fires for archive because the page stops being enumerated — the orphan pass is the load-bearing path. Realness proven by guard inversion (orphan pass disabled on the lightweight kind → red with orphans_deleted=0 → restored, reconciliation.py sha256 byte-identical, git clean). A new pure domain module retrieval/domain/rerank_enrichment.py builds (a) a one-line verified-context preamble prepended to the query string (POS/PMS/Accounting slot product ids + host platform key — public catalog vocab only, never a company id/token/secret) and (b) a short YAML applicability block ahead of each document's text, derived from the chunk's stamped applicability payload + the §D.4 matcher's matched-reason slug (no new vocabulary); HybridRetriever._build_rerank_inputs wires it in — only when a verified CustomerContext is present, so the no-context/internal/eval path is byte-identical to pre-8.3.2; fetch_rerank_texts selects the applicability column ONLY when eligibility is present (so the no-context SQL stays byte-for-byte and the 8.3.1 test_rt2_filter_sql_unchanged stays green unmodified); RerankCandidate gained an applicability field (Sequence[Mapping]|None, default None); caps per the memo — the query ≤ 2,048 tokens with the user question NEVER truncated (the preamble yields first; token length estimated conservatively at ~4 chars/token, no tokenizer added to the v2 client), the YAML metadata-first inside the 4,000-char document cap so it always survives (body tail truncated after it); eligibility stays enforced before rerank and is never widened — the enrichment is built only from the already-eligible candidate set fetch_rerank_texts returned · security (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints, touched surface = the Cohere rerank outbound call + its request construction): no new endpoint; the CohereReranker transport (C4 timeout/retry/breaker, C10 doc cap) is UNCHANGED, its breaker/retry/abuse-cap tests stay green; injection — the page-authored "Applies to" values are validated against the catalog vocabulary at stamp time (8.2.1) AND additionally sanitized in _scalar (newlines/CR/control chars collapsed, length bounded), serialized only into a cross-encoder document string, never executed, never interpolated into SQL (hostile-value unit test proves no injected second YAML line); rule 10 — the query preamble emits only public slot product ids + the platform key, never a company id/token/secret, and no new log or trace line is added (trace_repo untouched: still rerank_scores + reranker_model + page/chunk ids); TypeError — RerankCandidate had no applicability field), then a scripted metadata-first break-it (body-first) turned test_rt4_rerank_yaml_metadata_leads red, restored green; (2) the contract test was UPDATED, not added — the reranker client is a thin v2 transport, so the enrichment is built upstream in the retriever and the test now pins that the transport forwards the enriched query/documents verbatim while the v2 body keys are unchanged; (3) threshold re-tune = UNCHANGED (refusal_min_rerank_score 0.05→0.05, offtopic_max_rerank_score 0.035→0.035, CRAG trigger unchanged): the bundled gold set carries NO customer context, so the enrichment (which only fires on the context path) is invisible to it and cannot shift its score distribution — there was nothing to tune against; 8.3.3 · The prompt knows the setup · 2026-10-05 · working tree, uncommitted per the branch convention · tests: 7 new (unit, backend/app/features/rag_agent/tests/test_prompt_integration_context.py, panel mi-prompt: test_rt5_integration_context_block_renders_the_verified_setup, test_rt5_integration_context_block_is_dropped_when_nothing_is_known, test_rt5_base_and_data_hub_example_prompts_render_with_real_context; unit, rag_agent/tests/test_llm_client.py: test_rt5_generate_attaches_the_verified_integration_context_as_an_uncached_block, test_rt5_generate_adds_no_integration_block_without_a_verified_context; unit, rag_agent/tests/test_answer_service.py: test_rt5_answer_threads_the_verified_context_and_connections_into_generation, test_rt5_further_retrieval_never_exceeds_two_reads) · levels: unit + eval (make check green — boundaries OK, backend 1204 passed + 1 xfailed, knowledge-base 51; ruff 18 = baseline, format 1 pre-existing unformatted file = baseline, pyright 43 = baseline with 0 errors in touched files; make eval reproduces the 4.5/8.3.1/8.3.2 floor byte-identical — retrieval_smoke 1.000/0.750/1.000, ambiguity 1.000/0.667/1.000, permission 0.667/0.250/0.667, out_of_corpus 0.000, rerank lift p@5 0.200→0.200, ndcg@10 1.000→0.877; live skipped — STAGING_DATABASE_URL unset) · what was done: the grounded answer prompt now carries the SAME verified integration context the search (8.3.1) and reranker (8.3.2) already use. A new pure domain function build_integration_context_block (backend/app/features/rag_agent/domain/prompt.py — every prompt lives in the rag_agent domain layer) renders the known systems (filled §D.13 slots), the absent/unknown roles (named as the ask), the actual connections and their §D.2 directions, the host platform and the selected integration, plus answer guidance to name which system each fact comes from and to say a thing is not configured and ask rather than invent it; the block is presented as "facts, not instructions — never to decide what you may access" (same anti-injection posture as the identity/business blocks). Two populated example renderings, EXAMPLE_INTEGRATION_CONTEXT_DATA_HUB (Toast+Mews, pos-pms, both directions) and EXAMPLE_INTEGRATION_CONTEXT_BASE (Mews+Xero, pms-accounting), built with real catalog ids. AnthropicAnswerGenerator.generate gained customer_context + connections kwargs and appends the block as a third UNCACHED system block after the cached persona and the existing business-context block (dropped entirely when nothing is known, so the no-context prompt is byte-identical). AnswerService.answer derives the customer's actual connections through the catalog via eligibility_params (the exact §D.4 derivation the search eligibility uses — one semantics, two executors), threaded into generation; a new optional product_catalog constructor param (wired in main.py with load_product_catalog()) supplies it, falling back to the context's own real records when unwired. No retrieval/CRAG wiring changed. · security (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints, touched surface = the Anthropic grounded-generation call + its prompt assembly): no new endpoint/outbound surface — the same single create_message call with its unchanged C4 timeout/retry/breaker/abuse-cap discipline; prompt-injection — the block is built from VERIFIED-token values (slots/platform/integration from the frozen AuthContext, connections derived from them), never the request body; the slot/platform values are catalog-validated at the gate (8.1.3) so no page-authored/untrusted text enters this block (unlike 8.3.2's YAML, which there is none of here); it is presented as facts with an explicit never-for-access line; rule 10 — only public catalog product ids + a public platform key + the selected integration, no company id, token or secret, no new log/trace line; rule 3 — eligibility stays enforced by the search SQL + reranker (the chunks read are already eligibility-filtered); the prompt never widens access and a frontend selection cannot expand permissions; rule 7 — the reply still passes enforce_citations (cite-or-refuse) unchanged; rule 8 — no new read, CRAG retry still at most once (asserted by the read-counter test); C6 — the user_text (question+evidence) still goes through redact_pii, the system blocks carry no PII · deviations: (1) red-first: the prompt test failed at collection (ImportError — build_integration_context_block/example constants did not exist), the two generate/answer-service new-behavior tests failed (TypeError — generate/AnswerService had no such kwargs/param); the Break-it line was exercised by removing the integration-block injection in generate(), which turned test_rt5_generate_attaches...uncached_block red (StopIteration), then restored green; (2) test_rt5_further_retrieval_never_exceeds_two_reads is a REGRESSION GUARD for rule 8, green before and after — the prompt change touches no retrieval/CRAG wiring, so it cannot be red-first; it asserts the existing two-reads cap (initial + one CRAG retry) still holds with the context threaded through, proving no loop was introduced; (3) make eval ran AFTER only (no fresh BEFORE on unchanged code) because the bundled gold set carries NO customer context, so the integration block is never added and the no-context prompt is byte-identical — proven at unit level (test_rt5_...is_dropped_when_nothing_is_known, test_rt5_generate_adds_no_integration_block_without_a_verified_context); the AFTER run reproducing the floor is itself the before==after evidence; the one committed-report JSON delta (fixture page 3011 in the ranked tail) is pre-existing, metrics unchanged, report reverted to committed (same as 8.3.2); (4) two existing per-chat context sources now coexist as uncached system blocks — the 2026-09-23 business-context block (business name + integration) and this integration setup block; the integration value appears in both, accepted as a minor, non-contradictory redundancy rather than removing the operator's business-name feature (both are facts-only, access-neutral); recorded choice: the block carries only public product/platform ids + the selected integration, mirroring 8.3.2's preamble posture, no company id; (5) four external generate() fakes (3 in confluence_sync/tests/test_answer_workflow.py, 1 in test_chat_endpoint.py) gained a **_: object catch-all to absorb the new kwargs — they implement the evolving AnswerGenerator protocol and drive context-free paths; (6) placement: no answer/generation/prompt component doc existed — docs/Final_docs/retrieval/answer-prompt.md was written from current code first (the retrieval README places rag_agent stages 1/5 docs here) and updated in this change; its prose status is Unverified (coded and unit/eval-proven, but no live token carries slots yet — Phase A 8.5.2 validates live), while the mi-prompt panel status word is "built" matching 8.3.1/8.3.2 · defaults built to: never-bend rule 8 stays as written (max two extra reads, the further-retrieval mechanism fits inside the cap — §D.13 item 5 / §D.7, owner RESOLVED); the prompt gives context + answer guidance only and the backend alone enforces eligibility (§D.7 invariant)

8.4.1 · Standalone presets · 2026-10-05 · working tree, uncommitted per the branch convention · tests: 5 new — backend 4 (unit, backend/app/features/rag_agent/tests/test_standalone_presets.py, panel mi-presets): test_widget_every_shipped_preset_passes_the_same_gate_as_a_real_note, test_widget_presets_cover_the_four_flow_types_plus_the_three_system_workflow, test_sec_a_preset_cannot_express_an_out_of_allowlist_context, test_sec_a_preset_cannot_express_a_role_mismatched_context; frontend 1 (browser, Playwright, frontend/e2e/standalone-presets.spec.ts): "switching presets starts a fresh chat and keeps the simulated-context marker visible" · levels: unit + browser · what was done: backend-validated standalone test presets (§D.8) for the four §D.2 flow types (pos_to_pms, pms_to_pos, pms_to_accounting, pos_to_accounting) plus the Toast/Mews/QuickBooks three-system workflow (§D.9 example 4). The standalone environment is the test harness itself, so standalone_registry vends every catalog product (gate check 4, vend rights, then trivially passes for catalog products) while the other four checks — shape, membership, role, launch allowlist — do the real validating verbatim; so a preset can never express a context the backend would refuse (proven: an opera-cloud-as-PMS preset raises SlotNotOnAllowlistError, a mews-in-POS preset raises InvalidSlotValueError). isLocalOrDevEnv re-exported from the embed feature root (no cross-feature deep import). · security (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints): no HTTP endpoint, LLM call, outbound network, or token construction is touched in 8.4.1 — the preset selector is a client-side simulation that adds NO request-body field (context threading is substep 8.4.2), so nothing new reaches the backend and the request body still never decides access (rule 2); preset validation flows through the same build_auth_context gate and never widens access (rule 3 — presets are inputs to the one authorization context, not a way around it), asserted by the two test_sec cases; the obi-standalone issuer is a fixed internal registry key, never a real trusted issuer and never a verified signature (validation builds the context from already-structured slots), so no connection string/key/number is invented (rule 12); the JSON carries only public catalog ids + pretend preset-* company ids, no secret (rule 10) · verify: make check green (boundaries OK, backend 1219 passed + 1 xfailed, kb 51); ruff 18 = baseline, pyright 43 = baseline (0 errors in touched files); ruff format --check reports 3 pre-existing unformatted files (test_ingestion_pipeline.py, test_answer_service.py, test_attribution.py — all from the uncommitted 8.3.x tail, NONE touched by 8.4.1; my three touched .py files are formatted); frontend pnpm test 195 chat tests green + tsc --noEmit clean; pnpm test:e2e 5 specs green (the new one + widget-mounts + 3 citation-attribution); eval NOT re-run — presets add no retrieval/ranking path (no vector/score/limit/prompt change), the gate-reuse is read-only validation; next lint is unconfigured for non-interactive runs in this env (pre-existing), tsc is the type gate used · deviations: (1) scope — the actual threading of the simulated context into the outgoing request/token is substep 8.4.2 ("the widget carries the extended context"), so 8.4.1 ships the backend-validated preset artifact + the dev-only selector (fresh-chat + marker) only; 8.4.2 · The widget carries the extended context · 2026-10-06 · working tree, uncommitted per the branch convention · tests: 11 new — frontend unit/jsdom 10: `frontend/src/features/embed/tests/iframe-bridge.test.ts` (panel mi-contract, 5) — "mi-contract · 8.4.2: a slot-shaped note (context.slots) is accepted and threaded like a legacy note" (the T6 opaque-JWT no-op), "mi-contract · 8.4.2: a renewal that changes a SLOT (not integration/company) resets the conversation", "mi-contract · 8.4.2: a renewal to the IDENTICAL slot-shaped context does NOT reset", "mi-contract · 8.4.2: crossing from a legacy note to a slot-shaped note resets the conversation", "sec · 8.4.2: a slot-shaped note is held in memory only — no web storage, cookie, or logged context"; `frontend/src/features/chat/tests/chat-session-provider.test.tsx` (panel mi-contract, 3) — "mi-contract · 8.4.2: carries the selected standalone preset's simulated context on the outgoing request", "mi-contract · 8.4.2: a legacy/real session (no preset) sends NO simulatedContext (byte-compatible)", "sec · 8.4.2: the simulated context is held in memory only — never written to storage, a cookie, or a log"; `frontend/src/features/chat/tests/validation.test.ts` (2) — "mi-contract · 8.4.2: passes a well-shaped simulatedContext through unchanged", "mi-contract · 8.4.2: rejects a malformed simulatedContext (shape check, never decides access)"; frontend browser 1: `frontend/e2e/standalone-presets.spec.ts` — "mi-contract · 8.4.2: the widget carries the selected preset's simulated context on the chat request" · levels: unit (vitest/jsdom) + browser (Playwright) · what was done: features/chat and features/embed now thread the extended §D.13 token/context shape, legacy notes kept byte-compatible. Contracts: `packages/contracts/src/index.ts` adds the `SimulatedContext` interface (company_id + three POS/PMS/Accounting slots) and the optional `ChatRequest.simulatedContext`, mirrored in `src/openapi/chat.yaml` (a `SimulatedContext` schema + the requestBody property) — DEV/standalone-harness-only and non-authoritative (the token-claims `context.slots` form itself + `Citation.attribution`/`CitationAttribution` were already in the tree from 8.1.2/8.3.4, untouched here). Chat UI: `ui/chat-session-provider.tsx` — when a standalone preset is selected (dev env only; the 8.4.1 selector) `sendMessage` attaches the preset's `simulatedContext` to the outgoing request; undefined in a real/legacy session so the request is byte-identical to today; held in React state only. Chat server/proxy: `server/validation.ts` shape-checks `simulatedContext` (reject-malformed only, the value never decides access — the backend re-checks against the catalog), `server/route-handlers.ts` forwards it to the backend in snake_case (`simulated_context`) so the dev harness does not 400 while the backend logs-and-ignores it in favour of the verified token. Embed bridge (T6): `embed/iframe-bridge.ts` `decodeScope`/`slotsSignature`/`sameScope` now read the untrusted display-only `context.slots` in addition to `integration`/`company_id`, so a slot-only renewal (same company, same integration) correctly resets the conversation; the note still rides `obi:token` as an opaque signed JWT string, so the richer claims change NO message type (the confirmed T6 no-op, decision-memo §D.12 item T6 — an old host sending the legacy payload is byte-compatible). · security (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints): no new HTTP endpoint or LLM call is introduced — 8.4.2 only adds one optional body field forwarded by the existing proxy. The body never decides access (rule 2): `simulatedContext` is non-authoritative, shape-checked in the proxy and logged-and-ignored by the backend gate in favour of the verified token (rule 3 — the integration context comes only from the verified note). The proxy's key handling is untouched: the host `CHAT_API_KEY` stays server-side as the outbound Authorization and is never exposed to the browser; the user token still rides `X-Obi-Token`. No secret is persisted or logged (rule 10): `simulatedContext` carries only pretend `preset-*` company ids + public catalog ids, and both the chat-side and embed-side memory-only tests assert nothing reaches sessionStorage/localStorage/a cookie/any console channel; the iframe bridge's slot signature is display-only (never trust-bearing) and the token value never reaches a log (BIT-9 still green). No connection string, key or number invented (rule 12). · verify: make check GREEN (boundaries exit 0 · backend 1224 passed + 1 xfailed · kb 51 passed); pyright 43 errors = baseline (0 in touched files — all 43 are the uncommitted backend tail, no Python touched by 8.4.2); ruff unchanged (no .py touched); frontend `pnpm --filter web test` 298 passed (my 4 touched test files = 93 pass in isolation; tsc --noEmit exit 0); `pnpm test:e2e` 6 specs passed (was 5 at 8.4.1 — the one new 8.4.2 spec); break-it: added a `console.log` of the simulated context in the provider → the chat-side no-persistence test went red on the leaked `preset-secret-co`, removed it → green. · deviations: (1) completed across TWO agent runs — a prior obi-implementer wrote the code + tests then crashed at the session limit while editing `frontend/src/features/chat/FEATURES.md`; this run VERIFIED the partial work (all green, no repair needed), finished FEATURES.md (the chat Contracts-consumed + Tests-location lines and a brand-new `## embed` feature block, which had never existed despite the file claiming to document every feature), updated the panel today-lines, regenerated the brief, and wrote the records. (2) the must-be-true clause "a slot-shaped note renders with attribution" is proven by the attribution render (`message-bubble.tsx` CitationItem/ATTRIBUTION_LABELS) + its 4 jsdom tests + 3 `citation-attribution.spec.ts` e2e specs — all of which were delivered and already recorded under substep 8.3.4 (they sit in the same uncommitted working tree); 8.4.2's own contribution is the token/context threading, so those 7 tests are NOT re-counted here. (3) `pnpm --filter web test` exits non-zero on a pre-existing jsdom async-teardown race in `loader.test.ts` (an unhandled `postMessage` "Cannot read properties of null (reading '_origin')" logged AFTER its 18 tests pass) — reproduced on `loader.test.ts` in isolation, `loader.ts`/`loader.test.ts` are unchanged from HEAD (last touched by commit 0e21ca4) and do not import any 8.4.2 file, so it is unrelated to this substep and left untouched (fixing an unchanged non-8.4.2 file is out of scope); all 298 tests themselves pass. (4) eval not re-run — 8.4.2 adds no retrieval/ranking/prompt path (no vector/score/limit change); the simulated context is a non-authoritative dev-harness input the backend ignores. · defaults built to: §D.13 keuze 4 (legacy single-integration note never dropped, stays byte-compatible) and the decision-memo T6 call (note rides `obi:token` as an opaque JWT, payload grows with no message-type change) — no "Decision needed" row existed for mi-contract/mi-attrib; status word stays "built" on both panels (frontend threading coded + unit/browser-proven; live Phase A validation remains 8.5.2).

8.5.1 · The worked examples reproduce their lists · 2026-10-06 · working tree, uncommitted per the branch convention · tests: 5 new (database) — `backend/app/features/retrieval/tests/test_worked_examples_db.py`, panels mi-validate/mi-match/mi-filter: test_rt2_example1_mews_invoices_missing_in_quickbooks, test_rt2_example2_toast_room_charges_missing_in_mews, test_rt2_example3_mews_guest_info_unavailable_in_toast, test_rt2_example4_restaurant_charges_not_reaching_quickbooks, test_rt2_example5_two_connections_never_cross_match_toast_mews · plus the eval gold set extended with a fifth dataset `backend/app/features/evaluation/datasets/mixed_connection.json` (5 §D.9 questions, mc-01..mc-05) wired into `run_baseline._DATASET_FILES` · levels: database + eval · what was done: each of the five §D.9 worked examples is now ONE end-to-end database test that seeds a small corpus whose stamped applicability payloads (built through the same `validate_applies_to`→`applies_to_payload` path as production) realise the example's include/exclude documentation categories, runs the REAL `HybridRetriever.retrieve_with_context` under the example's verified `CustomerContext`, and asserts BOTH the EXACT set of included page ids AND the EXACT set of excluded page ids (never a count) — with each hit's `attribution` (`general`/`pos`/`pms`/`accounting`/`pair`) asserted as the inspectable inclusion reason per result. Example 1 (Mews+QuickBooks customer): include {general, Mews, QuickBooks=accounting, Mews/QuickBooks pair}, exclude {Mews/Xero, Toast/Mews room-charge, Xero, Toast}. Example 2 (Toast+Mews): include {general, Toast, Mews, the Toast/Mews connection both directions}, exclude {Mews/QuickBooks, QuickBooks, direct Toast/QuickBooks}. Example 3 (Toast+Mews, PMS→POS guest-info emphasis): include {general, Toast, Mews, both directions of the connection}, exclude {Mews/Xero, Opera-Cloud}. Example 4 (full three-slot, both connections): include {general, Toast, Mews, QuickBooks, Toast/Mews pair, Mews/QuickBooks pair, three-system workflow}, exclude {direct Toast/QuickBooks} — the honesty clause (§5.2). Example 5 (Toast/OperaCloud + Mews/QuickBooks, modelled by two REAL ConnectionRecords + empty slots per context-contract §9): include {general, Toast/OperaCloud pair, Mews/QuickBooks pair}, exclude {Toast/Mews room-charge, Toast/Mews guest-info, the workflow, bare Mews, bare Toast} — the cross-matching guard, fails loudly if a Toast/Mews pair ever cross-matches from two unrelated connections. red-first: these validate already-landed 8.3.1–8.3.4 behavior, so per obi-test-writer rule 8 they pass on current code; red-first proven by the break-it inversion (dropped `_ELIGIBILITY_CLAUSE` in `search_repo._base_filters` → all five red, every excluded page leaking back incl. example 5's Toast/Mews 85184 → restored → green). ZERO production code changed (purely additive tests + fixtures + gold data + the one-line dataset-list wiring); no bug surfaced. verify: make check GREEN (boundaries exit 0 · backend 1229 passed + 1 xfailed · kb 51 passed); pyright 43 = baseline (0 in touched files); ruff check + ruff format --check clean on the two touched .py files (test file + run_baseline.py); make eval GREEN — the four existing stages byte-identical to the 4.5 floor, new `mixed_connection` stage recorded as its own first baseline (recall@5 1.000 · mrr 0.6167 · hit_rate@5 1.000 · p@5 0.200 · ndcg@5 0.7123), recorded in docs/plan/baseline.md §8.5.1 · security (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints): no HTTP endpoint, LLM call, outbound network or token construction touched — the five tests drive the in-process retriever against the local test DB with fakes, the eval dataset is static JSON; row security untouched (reader-role session, GUCs set by the retriever as in 8.3.1); no secret, connection string, key or number invented (rule 12); no body-decides-access path (rule 2) — context is the verified `CustomerContext`, never a request body · deviations: (1) ORDERING — the action plan lists 8.4.3 (owner's Railway frontend deploy) before 8.5.1, but 8.4.3 is owner-gated/blocked and 8.5.1 is backend-only (db + eval) and does NOT depend on the deploy, so 8.5.1 was done now and 8.4.3 is left for the owner; this is an intentional independent-substep reorder, NOT a skipped step. (2) The §D.9 examples are stated as documentation CATEGORIES, not page-id lists; the tests transcribe them into concrete fixture pages via a documented category→payload mapping (in the test-module docstring) — "accounting" guidance for a QuickBooks customer is realised as that customer's actual accounting product (QuickBooks Online), since the launch catalog carries no separate accounting-scope payload (catalog.md §5 / decision-memo O9); this is a faithful mapping, not a weakened assertion (exact page-id include AND exclude sets are asserted). (3) Example 5's two-PMS property is not expressible by the single-product-per-role launch token, so it is modelled by the `CustomerContext.connections` real-record shape (context-contract §9) rather than slots — the honest representation of "both products present somewhere, no Toast↔Mews connection". (4) EVAL honesty — the bundled gold runner scores a context-free baseline ranker, so the `mixed_connection` numbers gate retrieval RANKING of the questions, not eligibility; the exact include/exclude proof is the five db tests (same scope note as baseline.md §8.3.1/§8.3.2); the new questions are new, so "before" IS this run (no earlier floor to regress against), stated in §8.5.1. · defaults built to: no "Decision needed" row existed for mi-validate/mi-match/mi-filter at this substep; the category→page mapping and the real-connection-record modelling of example 5 are built to the settled context-contract (§5.2, §9) and catalog (decision-memo O9) defaults, named above. mi-validate stays status "build" (status word unchanged — the automated reproduction is the evidence/bar for Phase A human validation 8.5.2, which is owner-run and not yet done); mi-match/mi-filter stay "built", today-lines advanced.

3.6.7 · The screenshot drives retrieval · 2026-10-07 · working tree, uncommitted per the branch convention · tests: 12 new, 7 updated — backend unit 9: `backend/app/features/rag_agent/tests/test_answer_service.py` (panels r2-embed/r5-image/r1-limits) — test_rt2_image_transcription_folds_into_the_search_query, test_rt2_image_only_turn_now_retrieves_on_the_transcription, test_r5_small_talk_turn_with_an_image_still_analyzes_it, test_r5_clarification_turn_with_an_image_still_analyzes_it, test_r5_image_transcription_is_passed_as_context_but_never_a_citable_source, test_r5_two_read_cap_holds_with_an_image, test_sec_image_transcription_is_never_written_to_the_trace_query; `backend/app/features/rag_agent/tests/test_llm_client.py` (panel r5-image) — test_generate_image_analysis_uses_the_raised_transcription_token_budget, test_generate_image_analysis_prompt_is_transcription_grade; backend database 3: `backend/app/features/confluence_sync/tests/test_chat_endpoint.py` (panel r1-limits) — test_r1_limits_unsupported_image_media_type_is_rejected_400, test_r1_limits_each_allowed_image_media_type_is_accepted, test_r1_limits_tenth_image_accepted_eleventh_rejected; updated 7 (behavior-change, not new): test_curated_entries_still_compose_on_the_text_empty_image_only_path (now asserts the image-only turn retrieves on the transcription, not an empty result), test_image_on_turn_with_no_retrieved_candidates_does_not_refuse / test_image_analysis_survives_a_citation_enforcement_refusal / test_image_analysis_is_never_passed_through_citation_enforcement (retriever key now the folded query), test_image_only_turn_with_empty_content_skips_retrieval → renamed test_image_only_turn_with_no_text_and_no_transcription_skips_retrieval (the only remaining empty-query case), test_chat_image_caps_default_to_the_w_composer_images_decision (default 3→10), test_generate_image_analysis_caps_at_the_500_token_budget → renamed test_generate_image_analysis_uses_the_raised_transcription_token_budget · levels: unit + database + eval · what was done: the newest turn's image is transcribed FIRST (moved to the top of `AnswerService.answer`, before every short-circuit) and put to work three ways — (1) it folds into the ONE standalone search query (`_fold_search_query`: rewritten question + transcription) so the screenshot DRIVES retrieval; an image-only turn now searches on the transcription instead of building an empty `RetrievalResult()`; (2) it rides to grounded generation as an uncached CONTEXT block (`prompt.build_image_context_block`, passed via `generate(..., image_analysis=...)`), never folded into the numbered evidence, so it never gains a citation marker and never passes citation enforcement (rule 7 holds — every answer sentence still cites a real retrieved chunk); (3) it still rides back on `Answer.image_analysis`, delivered on `done`. A kept image is no longer silently dropped on a small-talk / identity / clarification turn (the transcription rides back on those short-circuit returns). C6 memory-only: `HybridRetriever.retrieve_with_context` gained an optional `trace_query` param — the search embeds/keyword-matches on the folded query, but the `query_trace` row records only the text-only query (`trace_query=rewritten`, and the verbatim text on the CRAG retry), so the transcription is never persisted/logged/traced. Two-read cap (rule 8) unchanged — the vision call is not a retrieval read; still one search + one CRAG retry (`_apply_crag_retry` now folds the same transcription into both the primary and verbatim queries and traces only the text). Prompt: `IMAGE_ANALYSIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT` repurposed from a free-form "answer from the image" into a transcription-grade extraction prompt (verbatim on-screen text + short UI description), Anthropic vision path only (no OCR engine, no other vendor), anti-injection sentence unchanged. `_IMAGE_ANALYSIS_MAX_TOKENS` raised 500→1500 (a dense screenshot's verbatim text runs past 500; output cap, not a latency/scaling figure). Caps: `chat_max_images_per_turn` 3→10 (`settings.py`), `MAX_ATTACHMENTS` 3→10 (`frontend/.../composer.tsx`), proxy `MAX_BODY_BYTES` 30_000_000→40_000_000 (`frontend/.../route-handlers.ts`). C3 allowlist: `_ALLOWED_IMAGE_MEDIA_TYPES={image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/gif}` in `router._validate_history`, 400 before any LLM call. · security (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints, LLM-CALL tier): C3 media-type allowlist ADDED (unsupported type → 400 before the vision call); C6 the image transcription is treated as PII and is memory-only — never logged, never written to the query_trace row (trace_query split), never persisted (asserted by test_sec_image_transcription_is_never_written_to_the_trace_query); C10 the vision tokens count against the same per-call spend discipline (timeout/retries/breaker in `AnthropicMessagesClient`, output capped by the raised `_IMAGE_ANALYSIS_MAX_TOKENS`); the body never decides access (rule 2 — images are content, scope still comes from the verified token); rule 8 two-read cap preserved; rule 7 citation integrity preserved (the transcription can never be cited); no secret/connection string/key/number invented (rule 12) — 10×3 MB base64 = 30 MB verified under Anthropic's documented 32 MB Messages-API request ceiling and far under its 100-images-per-request limit, the 40 MB proxy ceiling is the recorded decision. The disclosed image-bytes-not-PII-redacted gap (ADR-0009) and the live image-borne-injection adversarial pass (PLAN 7.6) are unchanged, still not solved here. · verify: make check GREEN (boundaries exit 0 · backend 1240 passed + 1 xfailed — was 1239 passed / 1 failed before the settings-default test was updated 3→10 · kb 51 passed); pyright 43 = baseline (0 errors in touched files); ruff check + ruff format --check clean on all touched .py files; make eval GREEN — the four pre-existing stages byte-identical to the recorded floor (retrieval_smoke recall@5 1.000 / mrr 0.750 / hit_rate@5 1.000; rerank ndcg@10 before 1.000 → after 0.877), proving no text-retrieval regression. · deviations: (1) EVAL honesty — the bundled gold runner carries NO image, so the image-driven retrieval path has NO eval coverage (documented gap, same honesty as the 8.3.x substeps); the image path is proven by the unit + database tests above, and the text gold set is byte-identical before/after. (2) scope — only `answer_service`/`prompt`/`llm_client`/`router`/`settings`/`retriever` + the two frontend caps were touched; the embed/iframe-bridge contract and the loader were NOT touched (the embedded host-page screenshot capture is a separate deferred substep). (3) "built" matches the panel convention used across Phase 8 (coded + unit/db-proven; the live image red-team remains PLAN 7.6). · defaults built to: decision w-composer-images (revised 2026-10-07: cap 3→10, 40 MB proxy ceiling, C3 allowlist, memory-only transcription, transcription drives retrieval without being citable) — built exactly to that revised decision; no open "Decision needed" row remained for these panels.

9.2.1 · Chat-start resolves and stores the session · 2026-10-12 · committed this session on explicit owner go-ahead (branch feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening; see git log for the hash) · tests: 5 (backend/app/features/rag_agent/tests/test_connection_block.py — test_rt1_connection_block_names_both_systems_and_direction, test_rt1_connection_block_unknown_system_says_so; backend/app/features/rag_agent/tests/test_chat_session.py — test_rt1_second_question_does_not_call_catalog, test_rt1_session_is_bound_to_its_subject, test_rt1_partner_switch_refires_and_replaces) · levels: unit + database — make check GREEN (boundaries exit 0 · backend 1275 passed + 1 xfailed · kb 53 passed); make eval unchanged (retrieval_smoke/ambiguity/permission/out_of_corpus identical to baseline, mixed_connection recall@5=1.000 — no metric fell); touched files clean under ruff + ruff format + pyright (my files add 0/0/0). What landed: migration 0017 (down_revision 0016_chunk_labels) creates chat_session (PK (subject_hash, company_id); columns business_id, source_slug/target_slug nullable, labels text[] DEFAULT '{}', connection_block, created_at, updated_at) and ENABLEs RLS with NO policy so the Supabase public REST roles are default-denied while the writer/owner bypasses (rule 1 / ADR-0013); its downgrade drops the table (reversible, rule 11). models.py gains the ChatSession ORM model (exported in __all__). prompt.py gains build_connection_block (new, rag_agent domain — every prompt lives here): a known connection renders both systems + roles + direction + business id + "answer for this connection only"; an unresolved source/target renders a plain "could not be recognized" block, never a fabricated system (chat-start step 5). chat_session.py (new, rag_agent/application; exported from the feature root as start_chat_session / load_chat_session / ChatSessionError): start_chat_session resolves source/target via the catalog once, build_labels + build_connection_block, and upserts the row keyed on sha256(token_subject)+company_id — create on first start, replace labels+block in place on a partner switch (D7); load_chat_session reads the row by key and makes NO catalog call, so a second question reuses the stored labels/block (rule 8). The composite key is the subject binding: a different subject resolves to a different key and can neither read nor mutate another's session; the subject is hashed before storage (rule 10). · security: no NEW HTTP endpoint, LLM call or outbound network — the chat endpoint is not yet wired to these functions (that is 9.3.1); the securing-http-and-llm-endpoints identity/secret controls that DO apply are satisfied — session keyed on the VERIFIED token_subject+company_id never the request body (rules 2–3), subject hashed not stored raw (rule 10), chat_session fenced from public roles by RLS (rule 1), catalog resolved once per chat not per question (rule 8). · deviations: (1) stage tag rt1 used (retrieval-stage-1 / the chat gate; mirrors the existing mi-gate tests) since none of in1–in4 fit; DB tests carry pytestmark = pytest.mark.db per this repo's actual convention. (2) Scope: 9.2.1 builds the mechanism + store + migration + prompt block + tests ONLY; it does NOT wire into POST /chat (the token does not yet carry source/target and the gate does not populate the session — that is 9.3.1) nor consume the stored block/labels in retrieval (9.4.1). The substep's Must-be-true/Proof are met in full (once-per-chat spy, cross-subject denial DB test, partner-switch refire); the "answer model consumes the stored block" line is set up but exercised end-to-end only once 9.3.1/9.4.1 wire it. (3) The cross-subject binding is enforced at the application layer by the composite primary key (mirroring _idempotency_cache_key), not by an RLS subject-GUC — the chat path reads/writes via the writer/owner engine which bypasses RLS, so an RLS predicate there would be theatre; the DB test proves the binding at the database level. (4) FIX to an unrelated latent bug: schema.enable/disable_non_chunk_rls iterate live ORM metadata, so adding chat_session (the first brand-new table after migration 0009) broke the kb 0007/0009 migration round-trip tests (ALTER TABLE on a not-yet-created table); guarded both helpers with a to_regclass existence check so the chain stays valid for any future table — minimal, behaviour-preserving. (5) committed this session on the owner's explicit full-engine go-ahead, departing from the branch's usual uncommitted-working-tree convention. (6) whole-repo ruff-format (4 untouched files) and pyright (46) sit above the stale 2026-10-05 baseline.md numbers (1 / 43), but that drift predates 9.2.1 and none is in a file this substep touched (verified: clean HEAD pyright is already 46; the 4 format-drift files are not mine). · defaults built to: ADR-0020 D4/D7 (direction-primary four boxes, one directed connection per chat) + chat-start steps 3/5 (connection block, unknown-system → [obi-general]); the connection-routing decision (decisions.md) is confirmed, so no open "Decision needed" row introduced.

What landed — CONTRACT: packages/contracts/src/token-claims.json gains the third optional form source_system + target_system + business_id (legacy integration and context.slots stay ACCEPTED but no longer route); the legacy all-three-or-none `if` becomes {"not":{"anyOf":[{required:["context"]},{required:["source_system"]}]}}; chat.yaml documents the directed form + the unknown source/target 400-before-search and adds `connection` to the Citation attribution enum; packages/contracts/src/index.ts adds the `connection` CitationAttribution variant. token_verifier.py: VerifiedClaims gains source_system/target_system/business_id, _parse_directed structurally validates them (missing counterpart / missing-empty business_id / non-string → bare TokenError 401), directed form takes precedence over context/legacy for the trio rule. The REQUIRED_CLAIMS drift test is unchanged (iss/aud/sub/iat/exp); a new drift test pins the directed fields published-and-optional, and the moved test_mi_contract_slots assertions pin the new `if` + the connection attribution across TS/OpenAPI. GATE: build_auth_context gains _build_directed_context (runs before the slot chain when source_system/target_system present) — resolves both via catalog.resolve_system (the SAME lenient resolver chat-start uses; unknown → UnknownIntegrationError = 400 before any search, §D.11), vend-rights check on both endpoints (IntegrationNotAllowedError = 401), slot role check (3) + launch allowlist (5) LAPSE (ADR-0020 D4/D5); business_id → AuthContext.company_id, resolved source_slug/target_slug carried for chat-start, scopes stay general-only (label hard filter is 9.4.1). _resolve_auth_context already maps the 400/401 families, so no router error-mapping change. WIDGET: iframe-bridge.ts TokenScope/decodeScope/sameScope gain source/target (company falls back to business_id) so a silent renewal that switches the connection — including a direction swap — fires onScopeChange → restart() and resets the thread (no cross-connection bleed); still memory-only, never storage/cookie/log (rule 10, existing sec test green). message-bubble.tsx renders the `connection` attribution label. Test-host mint: api/test-hosts/config.ts gains optional directedClaims (toast→mews, opera-cloud→toast) and [name]/obi-token/route.ts emits the directed form on `?form=directed`, legacy by default (byte-identical when not requested).
