9.4.1 · labels && session_labels, before top-k · 2026-10-12 · committed this session on explicit owner full-engine go-ahead (branch feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening; see git log for the hash) · tests: 15 new (backend/app/features/retrieval/tests/test_search_repo_labels.py — test_tags_keyword_search_omits_label_predicate_when_session_labels_is_none, test_tags_keyword_search_adds_label_predicate_when_session_labels_given, test_tags_dense_search_adds_label_predicate_when_session_labels_given, test_tags_fetch_rerank_texts_adds_label_predicate_when_session_labels_given, test_tags_fetch_rerank_texts_omits_label_predicate_when_session_labels_is_none, test_sec_malicious_label_value_never_reaches_sql_text, test_sec_apply_allowed_labels_uses_bound_param_not_interpolated, test_sec_apply_allowed_labels_none_opts_out_with_wildcard; backend/app/features/retrieval/tests/test_label_hardfilter_db.py — test_tags_toast_mews_chat_never_sees_wrong_connection_at_any_similarity, test_tags_empty_labels_chunk_never_served_under_active_connection, test_tags_rerank_text_fetch_is_already_label_filtered, test_tags_recall_against_exact_filtered_baseline_records_no_loss, test_tags_legacy_token_without_session_labels_is_a_no_op; backend/app/features/rag_agent/tests/test_answer_service.py — test_rt1_directed_chat_feeds_session_labels_to_filter_and_block_to_prompt, test_rt1_legacy_chat_routes_no_session_labels_or_connection_block) · levels: database + eval (+ unit for the SQL-shape and wiring tests) — boundaries exit 0; backend unit 909 passed + 1 xfailed, kb 29 passed; make test-db 390 passed + 1 xfailed + kb 24 passed; make eval byte-identical to the floor (retrieval_smoke 1.000/0.750/1.000 · ambiguity 1.000/0.667/1.000 · permission 0.667/0.250/0.667 · out_of_corpus 0.000 · mixed_connection 1.000/0.617/1.000; rerank lift p@5 0.200→0.200, ndcg@10 1.000→0.877 — no metric fell); touched files clean under ruff + ruff format + pyright (my files 0/0/0). What landed — RETRIEVAL: search_repo.py gains apply_allowed_labels (sets the app.allowed_labels GUC via a bound set_config; None → the '*' opt-out, a list → joined+bound, an element carrying ',' or '*' rejected before the join) and a session_labels param on _base_filters/keyword_search/dense_search/fetch_rerank_texts that adds AND labels && :session_labels at the exact spot of the tags overlap (bound, never interpolated), threaded identically through both candidate queries AND the rerank-text fetch — so an excluded page can never be reranked in; omitted entirely when None (byte-for-byte the pre-9.4.1 SQL). retriever.py: _search sets apply_allowed_labels in the SAME transaction/path as apply_source_scope + apply_knowledge_scope (so the RESTRICTIVE chunk_labels_read RLS policy from migration 0016 and the app predicate agree — rules 1 and 3), passes session_labels to all three searches (via a single dict[str,Any] search_kwargs that also carries eligibility, so the no-connection/no-context call shape stays byte-identical for the repo-function test doubles); retrieve/retrieve_with_context gain session_labels; fetch_parent_texts sets the labels GUC to '*' (parent expansion is label-EXEMPT — the matched child already passed the filter, D1/D2, panel mi-hardfilter). WIRING into POST /chat (deferred from 9.3.1): answer_service.py gains _resolve_directed_routing — for a directed source→target chat it loads this subject's chat_session once and, on the first question or a partner switch (a different source/target than the stored row), calls start_chat_session (the one catalog lookup) else reuses the stored row with no catalog call (rule 8); the stored labels become session_labels for retrieve_with_context AND the CRAG retry, and connection_block is threaded into generate; a legacy/slot/tokenless chat resolves to (None,None) so the filter is a no-op and the prompt carries no block. llm_client.py: AnswerGenerator.generate + AnthropicAnswerGenerator.generate gain connection_block, rendered as an uncached per-chat system block (facts, not instructions; response context only, access still governed by the filter + locks). main.py wires session_store_sessionmaker=get_sessionmaker() (writer). · security (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints — this touches the /chat answer path + retrieval SQL): PASS — the connection/labels come ONLY from the verified token/session row, never the request body (rules 2–3; the body model still forbids source/target via extra=forbid); labels ride as bound params, never interpolated, and a value carrying ',' or '*' is rejected before the join (rule 10); no new corpus read — the session load is a keyed lookup and parent expansion stays exempt, so at most two extra reads per question is unchanged (rule 8); the chunk_labels_read RESTRICTIVE policy becomes LIVE and ANDs with the source + scope policies (rule 1 — never disabled); labels are fail-OPEN on an unset/opt-out GUC by design (the relevance axis, not the access fence — source isolation lock 1 and customer-scope isolation lock 2 stay fail-closed and UNCHANGED); nothing sensitive logged — labels and the connection block are public catalog vocabulary + the already-carried business_id, no secret in SQL/logs/traces (rule 10). No migration in this substep (the labels column + RLS policy landed in 0016/9.1.2). · deviations / less than the full substep (unprompted): (1) ADR-0020 D0 reconciliation (item 4) required NO code or config change — a page carrying a routing label + the base obi-general-test membership tag already indexes (obi-general-test is a recognized scope in knowledge_scopes.json, always allowed) and already routes (the directed gate's _build_directed_context sets allowed_scopes=(obi-general-test,), which the scope filter admits, and the routing labels then filter it); no per-integration obi-*-test tag is needed. Verified by reasoning over the allowlist + the gate code, not by a live relabelled corpus (that is 9.5.1). (2) make eval proves no-regression on the no-connection path ONLY — the bundled gold set carries no routing labels, so the filter is invisible to it (its SQL is byte-identical without session_labels); the formal routing-off-vs-on labelled before/after dataset is substep 9.5.2, explicitly NOT built here per the task. The filter's own recall/exclusion proof is the database-level test_label_hardfilter_db.py (exact include/exclude + exact-filtered-baseline recall + rerank-input-already-filtered + empty-labels-never-served), break-it-inverted red-first. (3) apply_allowed_labels is internal to the retrieval feature (not re-exported from the feature root), consistent with keyword_search/dense_search/fetch_rerank_texts which are also internal — only the retriever and the feature's own tests use it. (4) five existing retrieval unit tests needed updating: four (test_permission_filter, test_rerank_context_wiring, test_torerank_handoff, test_retrieval_handoff_contract) gained a one-line apply_allowed_labels no-op monkeypatch mirroring their existing apply_knowledge_scope monkeypatch (the new GUC call runs in _search against their fake sessions), and test_s_reader_session_binding gained an assertion that the labels GUC binds to the SAME reader session as source + scope (reinforcing rule 3) — minimal, behaviour-preserving. (5) panel mi-hardfilter set to UNVERIFIED not built: coded + db + eval proven, but not proven on a relabelled live corpus (9.5.1) or a live token flow — honest per the "words with one meaning" definitions. (6) a single `make check` errors all DB tests (its test target runs against the root .env Supabase host-guard without provisioning the local DB) — the authoritative DB level is make test-db (local pinned pgvector), which is GREEN; a pre-existing harness split, not a code failure. (7) whole-repo ruff-format (untouched files) and pyright (46) sit above the stale 2026-10-05 baseline.md numbers (1 / 43); that drift predates 9.4.1 and none is in a file this substep touched (my files are 0/0/0, verified absent from the pyright error list). · defaults built to: ADR-0020 D1 (two-layer GUC + RESTRICTIVE RLS policy + app predicate), D2 (empty-labels chunk invisible under an active connection), D6 (a chat reads only its four boxes) — all confirmed; the connection-routing decision (docs/plan/decisions.md) is settled, so no open "Decision needed" row introduced.

9.5.2 · Eval before and after routing · 2026-10-12 · committed this session on explicit owner per-substep go-ahead (branch feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening; see git log for the hash) · tests: 5 new (backend/app/features/evaluation/tests/test_routing_lift.py — test_eval_routing_on_narrows_while_recall_holds, test_eval_routing_no_lift_when_labels_change_nothing, test_eval_routing_empty_dataset_is_safe; backend/app/features/retrieval/tests/test_eval_routing_db.py — test_eval_routing_off_vs_on_records_no_loss_and_narrows, test_eval_routing_off_vs_on_break_it_no_labels_is_zero_lift) · levels: eval + database (+ unit for the harness math) — boundaries exit 0; backend unit 912 passed, kb 29 passed; make test-db 392 passed + 1 xfailed + kb 24 passed; make eval prints the routing-off-vs-on table AND leaves the bundled floor byte-identical (retrieval_smoke 1.000/0.750/1.000 · ambiguity 1.000/0.667/1.000 · permission 0.667/0.250/0.667 · out_of_corpus 0.000 · mixed_connection 1.000/0.617/1.000; rerank lift p@5 0.200→0.200, ndcg@10 1.000→0.877 — no metric fell); touched files clean under ruff + ruff format + pyright (my 6 files 0/0/0). ROUTING-OFF vs ROUTING-ON (connection_routing, 2 cases, k=50): recall 1.000→1.000 (no gold-set loss, rule 11), served_precision 0.167→0.333 (wrong-connection noise removed), served_count 6.0→3.0 (candidate set narrowed) — written to backend/eval-reports/routing_lift.{json,md}. What landed — EVAL: schemas.py adds EvalCase.session_labels (default []; every pre-9.5.2 dataset loads unchanged) + RoutingLiftReport (recall/served_precision/served_count, before=off/after=on/delta). runner.py adds evaluate_routing_lift(dataset, off_fn, on_fn, now_iso, *, retrieve_k=50) over set-based metrics (order-independent, so off/on never rides an internal tie-break) + the RoutingRankFn type. run_baseline.py adds write_routing_lift_reports + render_routing_lift_markdown + _print_saved_routing_lift (called from run(), mirroring the rerank-lift printer). __init__.py re-exports RoutingLiftReport/RoutingRankFn/evaluate_routing_lift/write_routing_lift_reports from the feature root. datasets/connection_routing.json — the labelled connection gold stage (2 directed cases: toast→mews, mews→quickbooks-online), each carrying its four session_labels. test_eval_routing_db.py seeds the labelled pages hermetically (mirrors test_label_hardfilter_db.py; each case in a private space_id so the two cases never contaminate), runs the real HybridRetriever labels-off then labels-on through evaluate_routing_lift, asserts recall held + served narrowed, and writes the committed report. · security (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints): NOT APPLICABLE — eval-only. No HTTP endpoint, no LLM call, no outbound network: the new code reads a bundled JSON dataset, runs the already-shipped retriever against the local test Postgres (tests only), and writes a local report file. The directed-label filter it exercises (bound params, 9.4.1) was security-reviewed in 9.4.1 and is unchanged here. · deviations / less than the full substep (unprompted): (1) the labelled gold stage is a NEW, separate dataset (connection_routing.json) rather than extra rows in mixed_connection.json, and it is deliberately NOT added to run_baseline's _DATASET_FILES — its relevant pages are seeded in the DB (the Confluence relabel is the parallel owner task 9.5.1, so they are not in the bundled fixture corpus), and the DB-free trivial baseline ranker would score them 0/0 and pollute baseline.md with a false regression. The routing numbers are therefore produced by the DB-backed harness and persisted to eval-reports/routing_lift.{json,md}, exactly as rerank_lift is, and make eval PRINTS the saved table (the plan's "(or add a dataset in _DATASET_FILES)" is read as "add a dataset file", honouring the no-false-regression intent). (2) the before/after metric is routing-off vs routing-on (not the rerank cross-encoder lift); it uses recall + served-set precision/count instead of precision@5/ndcg@10 because with one relevant page per case precision@5 is 0.2 regardless of narrowing, so served_precision/served_count are what actually show the candidate set shrinking — recall is the no-loss gate (rule 11). (3) the committed routing_lift.{json,md} was produced by running test_eval_routing_db.py (deterministic: fake embedder + fake reranker + seeded labels), re-verified identical by make test-db; make eval does not regenerate it (it is DB-free), it prints the saved file — same pattern as rerank_lift. (4) panels mi-hardfilter + mi-match kept at unverified / change (NOT built): the before/after gate now exists and is green, but the filter is still not proven on a relabeled live corpus or a live token flow (9.5.1) — honest per the "words with one meaning" definitions. (5) a single `make check` errors all DB tests (its test target runs against the root .env Supabase host-guard without provisioning the local DB); the authoritative DB level is make test-db (local pinned pgvector), GREEN — pre-existing harness split, not a code failure. (6) whole-repo ruff-format (untouched files) and pyright sit above the stale 2026-10-05 baseline.md numbers; that drift predates 9.5.2 and none is in a file this substep touched (my 6 files 0/0/0). · defaults built to: the connection-routing decision (docs/plan/decisions.md) + ADR-0020 (directed routing, hard label filter) — all confirmed, so no open "Decision needed" row introduced.

9.6.1-addendum · Directed-connection live isolation script · 2026-10-12 · committed this session (branch feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening; see git log for the hash) · what landed: backend/scripts/verify_directed_routing_live.py — a read-only operator tool (no hermetic test; needs a real DB + the rag_reader role, same ownership pattern as verify_knowledge_scope_live.py / page_state.py) that reproduces the directed gate+retriever session EXACTLY — the non-owner rag_reader role (so the RLS policies bite) with app.allowed_sources='confluence:default', app.allowed_knowledge_scopes='obi-general-test', app.allowed_labels=build_labels(source,target) — then a plain SELECT of active chunks and prints, per page, visible-vs-expected under the three RLS policies (source, customer-scope, routing-label). exit 0=pass / 1=fail (leak or RLS bypassed) / 2=empty-corpus or unknown system; --mirror also runs the reversed connection to prove the direction flip. · proven locally this session (not a claim of a live-prod run): seeded the routing-test marker scheme (obi-general / toast-general / mews-general / toast-to-pms / mews-to-pos / toast-to-accounting / empty-labels) into local pgvector via the test _seed_page helper and ran it — toast→mews PASS (sees obi-general/toast-general/mews-general/toast-to-pms, hides mews-to-pos/toast-to-accounting/empty), mews→toast mirror PASS (flips to mews-to-pos, hides toast-to-pms), exit 0; the RLS-bypass safety path correctly reports "RLS enforced: False" and RESULT: FAIL when the reader DSN is the owner role; the seeded rows were deleted afterwards (local left clean). · levels: operator script — its underlying RLS/filter behaviour is already exercised at the db level by test_label_hardfilter_db.py (same policies); the script itself is run by hand. touched file clean under ruff + ruff format + pyright (0/0/0). · security (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints): not a surface — read-only, no HTTP endpoint, no LLM call, no outbound network; sets only bound transaction-local set_config GUCs (never interpolated), prints no secret. · why: turns Gate 3 of docs/runbooks/phase-9-directed-routing-live-test.md into a single command; the live-against-prod run stays owner-blocked (needs prod access + the 9.5.1 relabel + prod migrate-to-head). · deviations: added after the 9.6.1 commit as an addendum tool (the substep's live run is owner-gated, so the tool is the agent-doable part); ADR-0021 Path B · re-spec the deploy-readiness security gate for Path B (failing-test-first) · 2026-10-13 · UNCOMMITTED working tree (branch feat/rag-phase-4-embed-security-hardening; committed with the rest of this session's work — see git log) · closes the one item the preceding audit-follow-up entry deliberately left FLAGGED-not-fixed: `setup_supabase.py check_deploy_readiness` still asserting the retired `chunk_scope_read`. Done as its own substep with a failing-test-first rewrite + a `securing-http-and-llm-endpoints` review (the gate guards the reader path). · what landed: **`backend/scripts/setup_supabase.py`** — `_SCOPE_RLS_POLICIES` → `_READINESS_POLICIES = (("chunk","chunk_source_read"), ("curated_knowledge_entry","curated_knowledge_entry_reader_read"))` + `_ROUTING_POLICY = ("chunk","chunk_labels_read")`. The gate now asserts the ACTUAL Path B fail-closed tenant fence — both reader-path policies present as **PERMISSIVE/SELECT** (flipped from the old RESTRICTIVE/SELECT check; verified against `schema.py`: `chunk_source_read` is PERMISSIVE keyed on `app.allowed_sources`, `curated_knowledge_entry_reader_read` is `FOR SELECT TO rag_reader USING(true)`) **AND** RLS ENABLED on both tables (a single PERMISSIVE SELECT policy + RLS enabled is a valid deny-by-default fence — unset GUC admits no rows, never-bend rule 1). `chunk_labels_read` is **reported but never asserted** (RESTRICTIVE routing axis, fail-OPEN by design — failing on it would re-introduce the false-fail). The retired `chunk_scope_read` / `curated_knowledge_entry_scope_read` are no longer asserted (0018 dropped them). The **X2 empty-`tags` census is REMOVED** (code + the retired rc 11 path): under Path B `chunk.tags` is inert for retrieval (routing rides `chunk.labels`), so an empty-`tags` active chunk is not "dark content" — the metric is meaningless. rc is now 0 (ready) / 10 (backstop gap); the STALE banner the audit-follow-up pass added is gone, replaced by the Path B docstring + comment block. · tests (failing-test-first): **`test_deploy_readiness_check.py`** fully rewritten (4 tests, outcomes-only — return code + printed catalog facts): (1) ready under Path B (reader RLS applied in-txn → rc 0 + the `chunk_source_read present/permissive_select/rls_enabled` line); (2) FAIL when `chunk_source_read` absent (drop_chunk_rls); (3) FAIL when chunk RLS disabled though the policy is present (independent RLS-enabled check); (4) FAIL when the curated reader policy absent. Discrimination proven red→green: all 4 FAILED against the stale impl (it printed `chunk_scope_read`/restrictive/the census) and PASS against the fix; each fence gap (missing source policy, RLS disabled, missing curated reader) still correctly fails the gate. All in-txn + rolled back. · levels: unit/DB — the readiness suite green; **`make check` GREEN (independently re-run by the main session): boundaries 0 · backend 1542 passed + 1 xfailed · kb 53 passed**; ruff check + ruff format + pyright clean on both touched files. · security (securing-http-and-llm-endpoints): VERDICT PASS, no blockers — the gate is a read-only operator CLI control (catalog reads of `pg_policies` + `pg_class.relrowsecurity`), not an HTTP/LLM surface, but it is the pre-deploy backstop for the reader path feeding `/chat`. C1 (authz boundary): PASS — it now correctly characterizes the Path B fence and no longer false-fails a correct store (which previously risked an operator disabling the gate to ship). Fail-safe posture PRESERVED (every gap → non-zero, refuses; never silently passes). Injection (rule 10): PASS — all dynamic values bound, policy/table names are module constants, DSN printed via `_mask()`. · flag for the owner (surfaced, NOT fixed): under Path B the curated reader policy is `USING(true)`, so `curated_knowledge_entry` has **no per-tenant DB-layer isolation** (0018 dropped its scope RESTRICTIVE policy; curated scoping is now app-layer only). This is a deliberate ADR-0021 consequence — the gate verifies what Path B defines (RLS on + a reader-only policy so anon/PostgREST roles stay fenced) and cannot assert per-tenant curated isolation at the DB layer. Curated content is general (not customer-isolated), so this is expected; the owner should confirm the curated app-layer scoping story is covered where curated is read. No empty-`labels` census was added (empty labels is fail-open/served-to-legacy, not dark — no evidence it belongs). · defaults built to: owner's Path B decision (ADR-0021); no "Decision needed" row introduced.
